 exists = c:CHI

 }
}

 chi_adm_imperial_law_civil = yes
 has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_active
 chi_adm_imperial_law_court_still_requests = yes
 NOT = {
  exists = imposed_law
 }
 NOT = {
  has_journal_entry = je_law_imposition
 }
}

 chi_adm_imperial_law_valid = yes
 chi_adm_imperial_law_adopted = no

  var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 1
  var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 5
  var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 7
  var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 8
  var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 9
  var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 12
 }
}

  chi_adm_imperial_law_supported_law = yes

  c:CHI ?= this

  has_journal_entry = je_law_imposition
 }
